### 2026-05-28 20:45:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Personal Income #Importance 1

US Personal Income Stalls in April — US personal income was unchanged month-over-month in April 2026, falling short of market expectations for a 0.4% increase and following a slightly downwardly revised 0.5% gain in March. The flat reading was largely due to a $61.7 billion drop in farm proprietors’ income, reflecting lower payments under the Farmer Bridge Assistance Program after applications closed in mid-April. Proprietors’ income with inventory valuation and capital consumption adjustments also fell by $57.8 billion. The decline was partly offset by a $39.6 billion increase in compensation, including a $32.4 billion rise in wages and salaries. Private-sector wages and salaries increased by $28.5 billion, while government wages and salaries rose by $3.9 billion. Meanwhile, disposable personal income fell 0.1%, or $19.9 billion, after rising 0.5% in the previous month. Real disposable personal income declined 0.5%, deepening from an upwardly revised 0.2% decrease in March.

### 2026-05-28 20:39:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Current Account #Importance 1

Canada Current Account Deficit Widens More Than Expected — Canada's current account deficit widened by C$6.2 billion to C$7.2 billion in the first quarter of 2026, missing forecasts of a C$4.7 billion gap and marking the 15th consecutive quarterly deficit. The investment income surplus narrowed by C$4.9 billion to C$2.5 billion, mainly due to lower direct investment income. Meanwhile, the trade deficit in goods widened by C$3.3 billion to C$7.7 billion as imports rose faster than exports. Goods imports increased 5.5% to a record C$211 billion, driven primarily by a 38.3% surge in metal and non-metallic mineral products, largely gold, amid sharply higher precious metal prices. In contrast, Canada’s trade surplus in services increased by C$0.8 billion to C$1.3 billion.

### 2026-05-28 20:38:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Durable Goods Orders #Importance 1

US Durable Goods Orders Surprise on the Upside — New orders for US-manufactured durable goods jumped by 7.9% from the previous month to $346 billion in April 2026, after an upwardly revised 1.3% rise in the prior month and largely beating forecasts of a 3.5% advance. This marked the strongest increase in new orders since May 2025, mainly reflecting higher demand for transportation equipment (+21.5%), particularly nondefense aircraft and parts (+165.9%); and capital goods (21%). Orders also increased for fabricated metal products (3.5%); primary metals (1.9%) and machinery (0.5%), but fell for computer and electronic products (-0.7%). Excluding transportation, new orders rose by 1.1%, the same pace as in March; while excluding defense, they surged by 8.1%, after a 0.3% decline previously. Meanwhile, orders for non-defense capital goods excluding aircraft, a closely watched proxy for business spending plans, fell by 1.1%, following a 3.9% rise in the prior month.

### 2026-05-28 20:37:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Personal Spending #Importance 3

US Personal Spending Rises 0.5% in April — US personal spending increased by 0.5% month-on-month in April 2026, or $111.1 billion, slowing from an upwardly revised 1% gain in March and matching market expectations. Spending on goods rose by $44.0 billion, primarily driven by a $28.8 billion surge in gasoline and other energy goods amid soaring energy prices tied to the Middle East conflict. Additional gains were seen in food and beverages ($9.5 billion), other nondurable goods ($8.2 billion), and recreational goods and vehicles ($8.1 billion), while motor vehicle spending fell by $9.2 billion. Spending on services climbed by $67.2 billion, led by housing and utilities ($22.7 billion), recreation services ($12.1 billion), food services and accommodations ($11.3 billion), and final expenditures of nonprofit institutions ($9.9 billion). Meanwhile, inflation-adjusted consumer spending edged up 0.1% in April, slowing from an upwardly revised 0.3% increase in March.

### 2026-05-28 20:36:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Initial Jobless Claims #Importance 1

US Initial Unemployment Claims Inch Higher — The number of people claiming unemployment benefits in the US rose by 5,000 to 215,000 on the third week of May, slightly above market expectations that it would rise by 1,000. In the meantime, continuing jobless claims rose by 15,000 to 1,786,000 on the second week of May, in line with expectations of 1,780,000. Despite the slight increases, the claim counts remained firmly below averages from the previous year and extended the period of stability since the declines in initial claims in the last months, maintaining the backdrop of a robust labor market and adding leeway for the Fed to remain restrictive. Meanwhile, initial claims filed by federal employees, which have been under scrutiny due to government shutdowns that have delayed unemployment insurance filing, were relatively unchanged at 427.

### 2026-05-28 20:32:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 3

US GDP Growth Rate Revised Lower in Q1 — The US economy expanded an annualized 1.6% in Q1 2026, up from 0.5% in Q4 but below 2% in the advance estimate, primarily reflecting downward revisions to investment and consumer spending. Consumer spending rose 1.4%, less than 1.6% in the advance estimate. The rise was mainly supported by demand for services (1.8%) while goods remained subdued (0.4%). Also, gross private domestic investment rose 7%, below the 8.7% reported in the advance estimate. Business investment in equipment surged 17.2%, while spending on intellectual property products increased 11.6%. In contrast, investment in structures fell 5.4%, and residential investment declined 6.2%. Meanwhile, net trade contributed negatively to GDP (-1.25 pp), as exports rose by 13.1% (vs 12.9% in the first estimate) while imports jumped 21.1% (vs 21.4%). Government spending rose 4.4%, in line with the initial estimate, recovering from a 5.6% contraction in Q4, as activity resumed following the end of the government shutdown.

### 2026-05-28 20:25:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 1

Brazil Producer Inflation Hits 2-Year High — Brazil's producer prices rose 2.63% in April 2026 from the previous month, accelerating from a 2.28% increase in March and marking the sharpest monthly rise since March 2022. Prices increased in 21 of the 24 industrial activities surveyed. Chemical products posted the largest increase, surging 9.91% amid disruptions to petrochemical supply chains and higher naphtha prices linked to Middle East tensions. Rubber and plastic products rose 7.31%, reflecting higher costs for petrochemical inputs, while prices in oil refining and biofuels climbed 6.44% on higher diesel and ethanol prices amid concerns over the Strait of Hormuz. Extractive industries also remained a major inflationary driver, with prices increasing 4.92%. On an annual basis, producer inflation accelerated to 1.07%, reversing the 1.63% decline recorded in March.

### 2026-05-28 20:13:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Mexico Jobless Rate Lower than Expected at 2.5% — Mexico's unemployment rate came in at 2.5% in April 2026, matching the level seen a year earlier and below the expected 2.7%. The number of unemployed individuals decreased by 135 thousand to 1.5 million, while the number of employed grew by 704 thousand to 60.6 million. The economically active population climbed by 873 thousand to 42.9 million, leading to a participation rate of 59.1%. Of those employed, 41.6 million (68.6%) were salaried workers, while 13.4 million (22.1%) were self-employed. By sector, services employed 26.8 million people (44.2%), followed by trade with 11.7 million (19.3%), manufacturing with 10 million (16.4%), agriculture with 6.1 million (10.1%), and construction with 5.2 million (8.6%). Among the unemployed, 10.9% had not completed secondary education, compared with 89.1% with higher education. Most were aged 25 to 44 (45.1%), followed by ages 15 to 24 (34.3%). On a seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate fell to 2.6% from 2.8% in the prior month

Brazil Unemployment Rate Rises Less than Expected — The unemployment rate in Brazil rose to 5.8% in the moving quarter that ended April of 2026 from 5.4% in the moving quarter ending in January. The increase was aligned with seasonal movements and below the market expectations of 5.9%, marking the lowest unemployment rate for an April quarter on record. The unemployed population rose by 8% from the earlier moving quarter to 6.3 million. In turn, net employment eased by 0.3% to 102.3 million. Still, average earnings inched higher to R$3,732 per month from R$3,719.

【國家標準時間及綜合原子時香港節點揭牌】香港天文臺與中國科學院國家授時中心28日在港舉行“國家標準時間及綜合原子時香港節點”揭牌儀式。香港天文臺透過首個香港節點為中科院國家授時中心綜合原子時的產生與保持提供原始測量資料，共同推動綜合原子時發展。 香港天文臺臺長陳栢緯致辭表示，香港天文臺自2025年9月27日與國家授時中心簽署合作協議後，雙方在時間測量、守時和授時服務方面積極發展合作專案。非常感謝國家授時中心為天文臺提供首個被動型氫原子鐘及標準時間傳輸裝置，經過雙方專業人員的努力，為香港引入既準確又穩定的國家時間源，對支撐香港授時服務作出貢獻，也為國家保持時間基準的能力出一份力。 中科院國家授時中心主任張首剛說，國家授時中心作為我國唯一專門從事時間頻率科學研究、承擔國家標準時間產生、保持與發播任務的核心機構，此次在香港佈局建設標準時間傳輸裝置，是國家授時中心推動港澳深度融入國家發展大局的具體舉措，也是推動香港參與國家時間基準產生的首次實踐。(新華社)

【Zimbabwe Classifies 14 Minerals As Strategic; Lithium Producers Say No Immediate Impact】Zimbabwe has designated 14 minerals, including lithium, nickel, cobalt and graphite, as strategic minerals subject to equity and export controls and established a principle that the state may enforce mandatory minimum ownership via designated special-purpose vehicles (SPVs). Zhongkuang Resources said after local checks the 'minimum shareholding' is the personal view of the minister of Mines and Mining Development, not formal policy or government position, and currently has no impact on the company; it added that if pursued the approach would likely apply only to new mines, not existing operations. Tianhua Xinneng said the change has no immediate impact on the company. Separately, Zimbabwe's lithium export restrictions this year have created a supply gap; domestic lithium ore supply in China may be tight in May–June, and market estimates say prices could rise toward 250,000 yuan/ton in the next 2–3 months.

US Futures Ease from Records — US equity futures inched down on Thursday as an increasingly strained truce between Iran and the US lifted energy prices. Contracts attached to the S&P 500 and the Dow were around 0.5% down from yesterday's record high, while the Nasdaq 100 to drop 0.7%. The US continued to strike Iranian targets near the Persian Gulf overnight, adding to skepticism over an incoming deal after the White House rejected Tehran's claim that it had a draft of a peace agreement that would restart trade through the Strait of Hormuz. The tech sector led the losses for a second session as markets assessed the sustainability of strong guidance for the AI sector, with Nvidia and Tesla down more than 1% premarket. More traditional sectors of the economy were also under pressure as hawkish comments from FOMC members lifted long-term yields. Snowflake surged 40% premarket on a strong outlook, although the result did not spread to other software companies. Salesforce fell 2% after its earnings report.

ECB Officials Were Open to a Rate Rise in April: Minutes — A number of ECB members viewed the April decision to keep rates unchanged as a close call and indicated they would have supported a rate hike had it been proposed, according to the latest ECB meeting minutes. Policymakers warned that the energy-driven supply shock was proving more persistent than previously expected, increasing the risk of broader and more entrenched inflationary pressures, while the war in the Middle East was seen as a key source of uncertainty for both inflation and growth. Members also acknowledged the increasingly difficult trade-off facing monetary policy, as slowing economic activity and weakening confidence coincided with rising inflation risks. Several officials noted that even with two projected rate hikes this year, inflation was still expected to remain slightly above the ECB’s target. While rates were ultimately left unchanged in April, the ECB reiterated its readiness to tighten policy further if needed.

【伊朗軍方：過去24小時有26艘船透過霍爾木茲海峽】當地時間28日，伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊海軍公共關係部發布公告稱，在過去24小時內，26艘商船和油輪在獲得許可並與革命衛隊海軍協調後，透過了霍爾木茲海峽的安全通道。 公告表示， 在霍爾木茲海峽航行，獲取許可並進行協調是必須的。正如先前所宣佈的，從其他航線透過將被視為干擾航行並將受到處置。昨晚，有幾艘船隻試圖透過篡改和關閉導航系統非法進入波斯灣。革命衛隊海軍在進行多次無線電警告後，攔截並叫停了其中兩艘船隻，其餘船隻則被迫折返。 公告表示， 美軍在該地區違反了停火協議，向班達爾阿巴斯市機場的空曠區域發射了幾枚導彈，未造成任何損失。但作為對這一侵犯行為的回應，美軍發起襲擊的源頭基地遭到了反擊。公告還表示“ 如果美軍再次採取此類行動，將面臨我們嚴厲的回應。霍爾木茲海峽的控制和管理完全由革命衛隊海軍負責，任何對該海峽的干擾都將遭到我們的果斷回應。” （央視新聞）

【State Council Pushes Eligible Urban Renewal Projects To Issue Infrastructure REITs And Asset Securitizations】The State Council issued the Urban Renewal "15th Five-Year" Plan, directing financial support for urban renewal. The plan will explore financing for self-directed upgrades of aging urban housing and in‑situ demolition-and-rebuild projects, actively attract private capital, and promote eligible urban renewal projects to issue infrastructure REITs and other real-estate asset securitizations. It also supports qualified urban-renewal firms issuing corporate bonds and medium-term notes. Funding will follow a beneficiary-pays principle to leverage market financing and construct a shared financing mechanism among government, market and residents; the plan also standardizes new government‑private partnership mechanisms, encourages private-sector participation in infrastructure construction and operation, and calls for cautious progress on public-utility price reform.

【State Council Calls For Reducing Tax And Fee Burdens In Land Consolidation, Exchange And Joint Development】The State Council issued the Urban Renewal 15th Five‑Year Plan, directing stronger measures to activate existing land stock and promote mixed-use spatial development. It mandates a positive list for mixed land development, tighter planning-control requirements, a land‑use control and planning‑permit system, and multi‑department parallel approvals with joint supervision to enable lawful conversion of land use and building functions. Projects that use existing land or property to develop state‑supported industries may receive a transitional policy preserving land‑user status and planning conditions for a limited period, in principle up to five years; after the transition, land‑use payments may be handled via lease or negotiated transfer. The plan encourages multi‑party redevelopment, calls for reasonable cuts to tax and fee burdens on land consolidation, exchange and joint development, standardizes long‑term leases, lease‑then‑transfer and flexible‑term supply, supports sale‑with‑plan land transfers, and says industrial and commercial land‑use right renewals will be advanced lawfully and prudently.

【State Council To Support Eligible Projects With Central Budget Investment And Other Measures】State Council issued the Urban Renewal 15th Five-Year Plan, directing fiscal and financial support to scale urban renewal. Central budgetary investment and central fiscal funds will back eligible projects. Local government special bonds may be used to finance eligible urban renewal construction and count as project equity. Local governments should raise fiscal contributions and consolidate/reallocate funds while meeting national obligations. Targeted tax and fee relief for urban renewal will be implemented. Financial institutions are to provide market-based financing within their legal business scope and must not create new implicit local government debt. 【State Council Orders City-Level Measures To Increase Improvement-Oriented Housing Supply And Regulate Rental Market】State Council issued an Urban Renewal 15th Five‑Year Plan requiring a full census of urban stock assets, classification and disposal of undeveloped land and ongoing projects, and reactivation of idle or low‑efficiency assets including old factories, commercial and office buildings, commodity housing and public housing. The plan calls for accelerating a new real‑estate development model and reforming core systems for property development, financing and sales; it directs city‑specific measures to boost improvement‑oriented housing supply and to standardize the housing rental market. It prioritizes expanding affordable housing and strengthening housing support for low‑income urban households, new migrants and young people. The plan encourages property developers to pivot into urban renewal, deepens housing provident fund reform to broaden eligible uses and include flexible workers, and tightens management and standardization of existing urban infrastructure assets.

【龔正會見國際海上保險聯盟主席弗雷德裡克・德內弗勒】上海市市長龔正今天（5月28日）會見了國際海上保險聯盟主席弗雷德裡克・德內弗勒一行。龔正說，推動航運保險高質量發展是上海國際金融中心、航運中心建設的重要內容。我們將著眼於建設航運風險管理中心，進一步提高航運保險承保能力和全球服務水平，期待與國際海上保險聯盟等國際組織深化互利合作。希望聯盟積極助力上海提升航運保險水平，支援上海在綠色航運保險、新型風險轉移產品等領域深入探索。同時，希望聯盟繼續支援上海深度融入全球航運保險體系，支援上海航運保險協會積極參與國際規則的制定，進一步深化與上海的人才交流合作，引薦更多國際保險、再保險機構來滬開展業務。上海將一如既往地打造市場化、法治化、國際化一流營商環境，支援全球航運保險機構和企業在滬實現更大發展。德內弗勒說，在上海航運保險協會的支援下，我們加深了與中國合作伙伴的聯絡。中國正積極服務全球航運活動，在貨運險、船舶險等方面發展迅速，航運等領域科技創新日新月異，期待在行業發展中傾聽更多來自中國和上海的聲音。上海的發展目標與我們高度契合，希望在航運業數字化、智慧化、綠色化發展等方面進一步深化合作、加強交流，期待取得豐碩成果。 (上海釋出)

【Meituan Report: China Outdoor Sports Participation To Exceed 800 Million Visits, 1 Trillion Yuan In 2025】A joint report by Beijing Sport University, the China Institute for Sports Development, the National Fitness Research Institute and Meituan Research Institute projects China’s outdoor sports participation will exceed 800 million visits in 2025 and consumer spending will top 1 trillion yuan. Core consumers are aged 20–44; women account for 54.6% of participants. By subsegment: skiing represents 91% of snow-sport consumption; on land, karting 41.8%, camping 24.3% and trampolining 14.2%; in water sports, rafting is 77.9%; aerial experiences cluster in paragliding (34.5%) and skydiving (33.8%).

【小鵬廣州工廠：6月要生產7000輛GX，“這是死命令”】小鵬集團(09868.HK)正在面臨有史以來最大的產能挑戰。預售39.98萬元，實際限時權益起售價26.98萬元，一刀砍掉13萬元後，小鵬官方宣佈小鵬GX在12小時內大定突破2.68萬輛。車fans資料顯示，小鵬GX上市72小時內，門店平均新增訂單30~50輛，新增進店量較平日增長300%。小鵬產品矩陣總負責人吳安飛表示，GX的交付週期已經到達8周以上；當天下午，GX的頂配純電旗艦配置已經增至15~17周。實地調研發現，主產小鵬GX的工廠是小鵬廣州黃埔區製造基地的南區。小鵬GX的產線在今年春節搭建，並預生產了1000輛~2000輛GX。實地調研的當天，南區工廠生產了186輛GX。一位小鵬生產基地員工說，按照小鵬現在的要求，如果中途沒出什麼意外，目前上市12小時內大定的2.68萬輛小鵬GX，能在7月前做完。6月，GX生產線成熟後單日產能將翻倍到300輛~400輛，且6月GX的生產目標是7000輛，“下了死命令，做不完就加班”。（21財經）

【Xpeng Guangzhou Plant Targets 7,000 GX In June; Management Says "This Is A Death Order"】Xpeng (09868.HK) faces its largest production challenge after a steep limited-time price cut for the GX. The model’s presale was 399,800 yuan; the time-limited starting price was set at 269,800 yuan, a 130,000-yuan reduction. Xpeng reported confirmed orders of more than 26,800 units within 12 hours. Dealer data show average new orders of 30–50 per store within 72 hours and store visits up about 300% versus normal. Xpeng’s product head said overall GX delivery windows are already over eight weeks; top-spec electric flagship variants are at 15–17 weeks. The GX line is at Xpeng’s Guangzhou Huangpu South manufacturing base; the line was installed this Lunar New Year and pre-produced roughly 1,000–2,000 units. Inspectors observed 186 GX units produced at the plant on the day of the visit. A plant employee said, barring setbacks, the 26,800 orders taken in 12 hours could be completed before July. Xpeng plans a June ramp to a daily GX output of 300–400 units and has set a June production target of 7,000 units; management reportedly ordered completion under the threat of mandatory overtime, calling it "a death order."

### 2026-05-28 18:31:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Italian Producer Prices Rise the Most Since Early 2023 — Italy’s producer prices surged 6.8% year-over-year in April 2026, the steepest increase since February 2023, driven by soaring energy prices linked to the Iran conflict. Domestic prices climbed 8.8%, up from 5.4% in March, while core inflation (excluding energy) accelerated to 2.0% from 1.5%. In foreign markets, prices rose 0.6% (0.9% in the euro area, 0.3% outside it). By sector, domestic prices saw the sharpest gains in coke and refined petroleum products (+79.1%), followed by chemicals and metallurgy (+5.6%). Abroad, coke and refined petroleum products led non-euro area growth (+32.2%), while other manufacturing and transportation equipment posted notable increases in both the euro area (+6.2%) and non-euro area (+11.6%). Month-over-month, producer prices rose 0.3% after a 4.4% surge in March.

【SAMR and NDRC Issue 2026 Guideline for AI Metrology System And Capability Building】China's State Administration for Market Regulation (SAMR) and National Development and Reform Commission (NDRC) jointly issued the 2026 Guideline on AI Metrology System and Capability Building, outlining a six-part framework covering basic support, general and core technologies, metrology technical standards, the metrology services industry and intelligence-enabled metrology to link lab innovation with industry deployment. The Guideline prioritizes reducing measurement uncertainty and improving AI trustworthiness, targeting algorithmic black boxes and weak decision explainability by directing R&D into internal-state monitoring and representation of AI systems and promoting metrology standards to make AI performance measurable, comparable and traceable.

【河南：“十五五”時期將在融入服務全國統一大市場程序中發揮更大作用】記者從河南省政府新聞辦舉行的“開局起步‘十五五’專題新聞釋出會”上獲悉，“十五五”時期，河南將破卡點、建樞紐、成支點，在縱深推進融入服務全國統一大市場程序中發揮更大作用。河南省委副書記、省長王凱介紹，河南最大的特點和優勢就是承東啟西、連南貫北，溝通四海、聯接八方，這種獨特的區位優勢、交通優勢，使河南在全國統一大市場建設中獨具特色。河南省委、省政府連續兩年召開“新春第一會”進行部署安排，在融入服務全國統一大市場建設上取得積極效果。破卡點上，河南將加快健全市場基礎制度規則，規範地方政府經濟促進行為，對各類經營主體一視同仁、平等對待，破除各種形式的地方保護和市場分割，有效降低制度性交易成本。建樞紐上，河南將推動“四個樞紐”建設：加快完善物流網，大力發展多式聯運，降低社會物流成本，建設物流通道樞紐；建設鄭州、洛陽消費中心城市和消費新業態新模式新場景試點城市，健全縣鄉村三級物流配送網路，建設聯通內外、貫通城鄉、對接產銷、高效順暢的消費商貿樞紐；做好“原字號”“老字號”“新字號”“外字號”強產業大文章，加快構建具有河南特色優勢的現代化產業體系，建設產業連結樞紐；抓好鄭州要素市場化配置綜合改革試點，加快建設國家資料要素綜合試驗區，建成公共資源交易“全省一張網”，建設要素配置樞紐。成支點上，河南將打造“三個支點”：拓展鄭州—盧森堡“空中絲綢之路”雙樞紐模式，提升中歐班列鄭州集結中心能級和班列拓線擴量，實現“空陸數海”四路協同並進，打造高質量共建“一帶一路”融匯支點；實施自貿試驗區提升戰略，壯大貨物貿易、服務貿易、數字貿易，讓更多環球好物、豫產優品跨越山海、行銷天下，打造對外貿易擢轉支點；對接國際高標準經貿規則，提高利用外資質量，有序推動豫企“出海”，打造國際投資合作支點。（新華社）

【中國是全球保險品牌價值增長“核心驅動力”】總部位於英國倫敦的品牌估值機構品牌金融集團28日釋出的《2026全球保險品牌價值100強》報告顯示，13家中國保險品牌入圍全球百強，中國是全球保險品牌價值增長的“核心驅動力”。 報告顯示，中國平安連續十年蟬聯全球最具價值保險品牌榜首，中國人壽、中國人保入圍全球十強。依託市場規模、消費者需求、長期政策紅利，中國成為全球保險品牌價值增長的重要來源。 報告還特別關注中外合資保險品牌，認為工銀安盛人壽等品牌將國內大型銀行集團的規模及渠道優勢與國際保險行業能力相結合，在中國不斷變革的保險市場中發揮更大作用。 報告指出，從全球來看，2026年全球保險行業品牌價值實現五年最大增長，同比上漲14%，百強品牌總價值超過6070億美元。 品牌金融集團調研發現，全球消費者對保險產品最核心的訴求包括值得信賴、價效比高、客戶服務優質、便捷易合作、產品選擇豐富等。 品牌金融集團中國區總裁陳憶登告訴記者：“中國保險市場巨大的內需潛力與企業出海戰略的雙重驅動，是品牌價值持續攀升的核心動力。隨著中國持續推進金融高水平對外開放，中國保險品牌有望在全球市場中進一步拓展影響力，在服務中國企業出海、護航共建‘一帶一路’合作等方面發揮更大作用。”(新華社)

### 2026-05-28 18:22:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 1

Ireland Retail Activity Falls 0.2% in April — Retail sales in Ireland fell by 0.2% month-on-month in April 2026, following a downwardly revised 0.5% decline in the previous month. The largest volume decreases were recorded in clothing, footwear, and textiles (-2.9%), fuel (-2.3%), department stores (-2%), and electrical goods (-2%). Meanwhile, the overall decline was partly offset by stronger gains in books, newspapers, and stationery (+4.6%), food, beverages, and tobacco in specialised stores (+3.9%), pharmaceuticals, medical and cosmetic articles (+3.6%), and bars (+1.8%). On an annual basis, retail activity declined by 0.5% in April, reversing a downwardly revised 0.9% increase recorded in the preceding month.

### 2026-05-28 18:19:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macedonia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Macedonia Producer Inflation at 10-Month High — The annual producer inflation rate in North Macedonia rose by 5.1% in April 2026 from 4.4% in the previous month, reaching its highest level since June 2025. Producer prices continued to rise for capital goods (11.1% vs 7.8% in March), consumer goods (3.1% vs 2.5%), and non-durable goods (3% vs 2.3%). Meanwhile, inflation eased slightly for energy (10.1% vs 10.3%) and durable goods (5.2% vs 5.9%). By sectors and divisions, manufacturing prices increased further (4.4% vs 3.6%), driven mainly by higher prices for wearing apparel, leather and related products, chemicals and chemical products, computer, electronic and optical products, and machinery and equipment. In contrast, price growth slowed in mining and quarrying (14.9% vs 15.7%) and electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply (10.5% vs 10.7%), while it remained unchanged for water collection, treatment and supply at 6.4%. On a monthly basis, producer prices edged up 0.6% in April, following a 0.5% increase in March.

【Hisense Says Talks With LG Over TV Unit Are Routine; Sale Speculation Unconfirmed】An industry insider close to Hisense said recent coordination talks between LG Electronics and Hisense executives over business restructuring — including the reported possibility of LG selling its TV division — were routine exchanges and that other reports are speculative. In late March TCL Electronics signed an agreement with Sony to form a joint venture that will take over Sony’s TV and home-audio hardware business, with TCL Electronics as majority owner. Hisense previously acquired Toshiba’s TV operations. Analysts expect Chinese firms to further increase their share of the global TV market. Omdia consumer-electronics analyst Zhang Bing said a Hisense acquisition of LG’s TV unit is unlikely, noting LG ranked second by industry revenue in 2025 (behind Samsung) and was fourth by TV shipments last year after Samsung, TCL and Hisense, but he did not rule out resource-sharing or cooperation between LG and Chinese OEMs/brands in the TV supply chain.

【China's Large-Scale Mechanized Wheat Harvest Underway】As of 17:00 on May 28, the Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Affairs said 48.97 mln mu (≈3.26 mln ha) of summer wheat had been harvested nationwide, with daily mechanized harvesting exceeding 10 mln mu (≈666,000 ha). Overall progress is about 20%; the Southwest and Hubei are near completion, Henan and Anhui around 10%, and Jiangsu and Shaanxi have begun harvesting. The ministry said it will deploy more than 17 mln agricultural machines/implements nationwide and has coordinated with transport, police and meteorological authorities to ensure operational and logistical support.

### 2026-05-28 18:04:48  #其他

【江西公安釋出5起打擊查處涉煙花爆竹典型案例】5月28日，為嚴厲打擊震懾涉煙花爆竹違法犯罪，江西省公安廳釋出了5起打擊查處涉煙花爆竹典型案例。 施某豐、黃某根等人非法制造危險物質案。2025年2月起，施某豐在萍鄉蘆溪縣夥同黃某根非法制作“戰神”爆竹。其中施某豐負責提供原料、招工及銷售，黃某根負責提供配件、運輸貨物並收取運費。二人分兩批次生產銷售。蘆溪公安機關依法對施某豐、黃某根處以行政拘留十五日的處罰。 張某非法運輸爆炸物案。5月9日，萬載縣公安局在黃茅鎮鐵山界省際卡口盤查時發現，一輛私家車後備箱內裝有煙花內筒（含煙火藥）228個、約3.4公斤亮珠（煙火藥）。經查，張某因原工作的湖南瀏陽某生產企業停業整頓，遂聯絡到萬載縣黃茅鎮生產企業工作，在未取得任何許可的情況下，從原工作的瀏陽企業將上述危險物品運至新應聘的萬載縣黃茅鎮某生產企業做樣品試驗。目前張某已被依法刑事拘留。 李某危險作業案。5月19日，鄱陽縣公安局接群眾舉報線索，在李某家中查獲大量煙花爆竹，其中爆竹1744件、煙花518件。經查，李某於2024年11月4日取得煙花爆竹經營（零售）許可證後，將大量的煙花爆竹非法藏於離零售門店五公里外的家中。目前，李某因涉嫌危險作業罪被立案偵查，案件正在進一步偵辦中。 李某非法經營案。5月13日，吉水縣公安局查處一起非法經營刑事案件。經查，戴某父子二人為了牟取非法利益，2025年至2026年2月期間，僱傭李某負責進貨和銷售、僱傭曾某開車運送煙花爆竹，從萬載購買菸花爆竹非法運輸至吉安，在吉安市境內非法銷售批發煙花爆竹金額達200餘萬元。目前，李某、曾某兩人已被刑事拘留，案件正在進一步偵辦中。 梁某非法運輸危險物質案。5月16日，江西省公安廳交管總隊高速交警七支隊在泉南高速公路寧都南服務區開展治安巡查時，發現梁某駕駛的重型廂式貨車所載貨物為煙花爆竹。經查，梁某在未辦理煙花爆竹道路運輸許可證的情況下，非法運輸411件煙花爆竹。目前，梁某已被依法處以行政拘留處罰。（新華社）

Zambia Inflation Rate Cools Down for 5th Month — The annual inflation rate in Zambia fell to 6.6% in May 2026 from 6.8% in the prior month, marking the lowest since February 2018 and extending the slowdown to a fifth consecutive month. A stronger kwacha, boosted by rising copper prices and a bumper corn harvest, helped cushion inflationary pressures linked to the Middle East conflict. Food inflation moderated to 6.9% in May from 7.3% previously, while non-food price growth edged higher to 6.1% from 6%. On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.2%, after a 0.7% increase in the month before.

### 2026-05-28 17:58:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Croatia #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Croatia GDP Growth at Softest Pace Since 2020 — Croatia's economy expanded by 2.2% year-on-year in the first quarter of 2026, slowing from an upwardly revised 3.9% in the previous period and marking the weakest growth since a contraction in the fourth quarter of 2020. Government spending eased further (0.7% vs 2.8% in Q4 2025), while household consumption held steady at 2.6%. Net external demand also weighed on activity, as exports (-1.6% vs 1.5%) fell more than imports (-0.3% vs 0.3%). Gross fixed capital formation slowed to 2.5% from 7.0%. On the production side, growth softened in manufacturing (1.1% vs 6.4%), construction (2.8% vs 7.2%), wholesale and retail trade, transportation, storage, accommodation, and food service activities (1.8% vs 2.1%), and financial and insurance services (1.2% vs 2.9%). Meanwhile, stronger growth was seen in agriculture, forestry, and fishing (4.8% vs 1.7%) and public administration (3.0% vs 1.0%). On a seasonally adjusted quarterly basis, GDP was unchanged after a 1.2% rise in the previous period.

### 2026-05-28 17:52:11  #央行 #國際

【美聯儲古爾斯比：生產率提升的預期或誘發通脹 進而需要美聯儲加息】美聯儲古爾斯比重申，若市場預期未來生產率大幅提升，從而帶動投資和支出增加，可能會引發通脹，從而需要美聯儲提高利率。古爾斯比周四上午在東京出席日本央行一場會議時發表事先準備好的講話稱：“預期收入的增長，就像今天財富增加一樣：這會帶動支出增加，並可能在生產率繁榮真正到來之前就使經濟過熱。在這種情況下，利率可能需要上升”他表示：“對未來生產率的炒作越強烈，為防止經濟過熱，利率就越可能需要上調”古爾斯比還說，供給衝擊可能會加劇由未來生產率增長預期帶來的通脹在接受媒體另一場採訪時，古爾斯比表示，儘管他當然希望當前由中東能源衝擊引發的這輪通脹只是暫時現象，但他“並不完全確信”一定如此

### 2026-05-28 17:45:34  #宏觀

【聚焦涉未成年人保護熱點難點問題，最高檢釋出典型案例】記者5月28日從最高人民檢察院獲悉，最高檢當天釋出了“強化檢察監督，促進未成年人犯罪預防和治理”典型案例。這批典型案例共10件，聚焦新型毒品、網路犯罪、強制報告等當前社會關注的涉未成年人保護熱點難點問題。“拉幫結派”和“反覆作案”是未成年人犯罪中的常見現象，也是幫教矯治的痛點。在趙某某等人聚眾鬥毆案中，針對青少年為爭奪“老大”引發的群體性暴力，浙江省湖州市檢察機關開展分級干預。在王某某等人盜竊案中，檢察機關以案促治，協同構建犯罪預防體系，積極推動從源頭上降低因失學失管引發的犯罪風險。新型成癮性物質濫用是近年來未成年人保護工作中的突出問題。在於某某等人非法經營案中，針對“笑氣”等新型成癮性物質治理盲區，檢察機關立足辦案提出地方立法建議，推動省級層面填補立法空白。朱某某幫助資訊網路犯罪活動案與劉某某非法獲取計算機資訊系統資料案，則揭示了數字時代未成年人網路犯罪的新特徵。前者針對銀行卡管理漏洞，江蘇省徐州市檢察機關以數字檢察手段斬斷“工具人”黑產鏈條；針對後者，北京市檢察機關透過數智賦能開展精準矯治，探索“技術向善”的引導路徑，幫助涉案未成年人迴歸正途。在本次釋出的方某猥褻兒童案中，辦案檢察院牽頭建立長三角一體化示範區跨域強制報告協作機制，實現強制報告制度區域協同落實。此次釋出的案例還著重強調了檢察依法辦案履職促推綜合治理的“由案到治”。段某尋釁滋事案中，檢察機關以“檢察建議+調研報告”推動出臺規範性檔案，從源頭上防範未成年人購買危險器具實施暴力犯罪。記者瞭解到，本批典型案例重在全面準確貫徹寬嚴相濟刑事政策，準確把握懲治與保護關係。檢察機關積極推動建立健全“輕重不同、各有側重、梯級銜接”的分級分類干預機制，在依法懲治的同時，加強個性化幫教矯治，並深挖案件暴露出的問題根源，以個案辦理推動社會治理，從源頭消除未成年人犯罪誘因。（新華社）

### 2026-05-28 17:43:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belgium #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Belgium Inflation Rate Edges Up in May — Belgium’s annual inflation edged up to 4.08% in May 2026, from 4.01% in April, marking the highest reading since January 2025. Energy prices increased by 11.2%, following a surge of 10.58% last month, while natural gas inflation edged down to 10.1% from 11.4%. Upward price pressures came mainly from housing and utilities (3.63% vs 3.02%) and transports (7.9% vs 7.8%), followed by restaurantes and hotels (5.85% vs 4.78%) and recreation and culture (8.07% versus 6.85%). In contrast, inflation eased for food (1.19% versus 1.89%), followed by clothing and footwear (2.26% vs 3%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.14% vs 2.46%), comunication (1.86% vs 3.49%) and furnishings (0.38% vs 0.95%). Meanwhile, inflation remained steady for education (2.4%) and health (6.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices declined 0.08%, reversing a 1.47% increase in April.

【U.S. Judge Allows TRUMP Order Tightening Mail Voting; Democrats Suffer Setback】A U.S. judge on Thursday refused to block an executive order signed by TRUMP on March 31 that tightens mail-in voting rules, a legal setback for Democrats whose lawyers had argued the order could disenfranchise millions of voters. The order directs federal agencies to compile a confirmed-citizen list identifying state-eligible voters, instructs agencies to use federal data to help state election officials verify voter eligibility, directs the U.S. Postal Service to deliver ballots only to voters on state-approved mail-ballot lists, and requires states to retain election-related records for five years. The ruling comes as Republicans contest fiercely to retain control of both chambers of Congress in the November midterm elections; TRUMP has long alleged widespread fraud in the 2020 election and criticized mail voting.

【國家稅務總局秦皇島市稅務局稽查局依法查處秦皇島城市人家裝飾工程有限公司私戶收款偷稅案件】近期，國家稅務總局秦皇島市稅務局稽查局根據舉報線索，依法查處秦皇島城市人家裝飾工程有限公司私戶收款偷稅案件。經查，2020年至2023年，秦皇島城市人家裝飾工程有限公司透過實際控制人等私人賬戶收款的方式隱匿銷售收入，進行虛假申報，少繳增值稅、企業所得稅等稅費共計570.58萬元。2025年11月，國家稅務總局秦皇島市稅務局稽查局依據《中華人民共和國稅收徵收管理法》等相關法律法規規定，對其作出追繳稅費款、加收滯納金並處罰款共計1167.87萬元的處理處罰決定。目前，涉案稅費款、罰款已追繳入庫630.31萬元，剩餘罰款及滯納金正按規定進一步追繳。國家稅務總局秦皇島市稅務局稽查局有關負責人表示，透過私戶收款隱匿收入、進行虛假納稅申報等方式偷逃稅，屬於稅收徵管法明確的違法行為。稅務部門將依法查處各類涉稅違法行為，維護稅法權威和公平正義的稅收環境，促進各類經營主體長期規範健康發展。（國家稅務總局）

Eurozone Consumers Slightly Less Pessimistic in May — Euro Area consumer confidence was at -19 in May 2026, up from an over three-year low of -20.6 in April and confirming preliminary estimates. However, it remains firmly in negative territory, with inflationary pressures stemming from the Middle East conflict sustaining expectations of potential rate hikes. In the broader European Union, consumer sentiment also improved to -18.2 from -19.9 the month before, as consumers turned less pessimistic regarding their future household financial situation, major purchases, and the general economic outlook, while their assessment of past financial conditions remained broadly stable.

### 2026-05-28 17:22:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Cyprus #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Cyprus Industrial Output Rebounds in March — Industrial production in Cyprus grew by 4.7% year-on-year in March 2026, reversing a 2.1% decline in the previous month. The rebound was mainly driven by a sharp increase in electricity supply (19.0% vs -13.0% in February), mining and quarrying (9.2% vs -2.1 %), and manufacturing (2.8% vs -1.7%), particularly the manufacture of food, beverages and tobacco products (3.5% vs -3.9%), rubber and plastic products (7.1% vs 1.9%), and other non-metallic mineral products (4.5% vs -4.2%). Meanwhile, output growth eased for water supply and materials recovery (3.9% vs 5.2%). In the first quarter of 2026, industrial output increased 1.5% compared to the corresponding period of the previous year.

### 2026-05-28 17:21:37  #公司 #國際

【兩家美國稀土生產商因“竊密”掐架】美國芒廷山口材料公司日前起訴美國稀土公司“竊取”關鍵技術。這兩家稀土生產商都受總統特朗普領導的聯邦政府支援。當地時間27日，有訊息稱，芒廷山口材料公司上週在得克薩斯州起訴美國稀土公司，指控後者“竊取”其為永磁體研發的技術，並在法庭檔案中稱美國稀土公司“屢次未能實現商業和業績目標，現在訴諸於竊取技術”。這起訴訟的核心爭議是芒廷山口材料公司擁有的一項旨在改進磁體“矯頑力”、即抗消磁能力的專利技術。該公司指控一名去年跳槽至美國稀土公司的前員工向新僱主公開了相關技術，而這些技術是耗費“數年”工作和“數百萬美元”投資的成果。芒廷山口材料公司還稱，美國稀土公司慣於從其他公司招募員工，“之後透過這些員工盜用商業機密”。美國稀土公司回應稱，芒廷山口材料公司的起訴“歪曲我們的企業、文化和人”，“將積極自辯”。美國政府去年7月收購芒廷山口材料公司15%股份，並與其簽署10年期協議，包括產品最低限價等條款。芒廷山口材料公司還與美國通用汽車公司達成供應磁體協議。芒廷山口材料公司執行長詹姆斯·利廷斯基本月13日表示，該公司位於加利福尼亞州的芒廷山口稀土礦將“很快”開始稀土分離作業。美國稀土公司計劃中的採礦作業尚處初級階段，瞄準2028年實現商業級規模開採。該公司今年收到美國政府約16億美元有條件資助，用於計劃中的得州稀土礦和俄克拉何馬州磁體廠。（央視新聞）

### 2026-05-28 17:19:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malta #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Malta GDP Expands at Softer 3.9% in Q1 — Malta’s economy expanded by 3.9% year-on-year in the first quarter of 2026, slowing notably from an upwardly revised 6.5% growth rate recorded in the previous quarter. The moderation was mainly driven by softer net trade contributions, as imports increased by 4.9%, outpacing the 5.6% rise in exports. Despite the slowdown, domestic demand remained a key driver of growth, contributing 3.6 percentage points to GDP, lifted by gains in both household consumption (3.8%) and government spending (8.5%). Moreover, investment activity strengthened as gross fixed capital formation increased by 3.6% in Q1.

### 2026-05-28 17:16:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Services Sentiment #Importance 1

Euro Area Services Sentiment Rebounds from 5-Year Low — The index measuring services confidence in the Euro Area rebounded to 2.2 from the upwardly revised, five-year low of 1.4 in the previous month, ahead of the market consensus that it would fall further to 0.2. Still, it was the second lowest reading in the five-year period, underscoring the impact of high energy prices faced by firms due to the halt of oil and gas supply from the Middle East. The current assessment of business situations eased its pessimism (-0.9 vs -2.4 in April), and the expected drop in employment also eased (-1.8 vs -4.9). Still, the evolution of demand worsened (2.6 vs 3.2). Signs energy prices may have already peaked supported the evolution of demand expected in the upcoming three months (4.9 vs 3.4), consistent with a drop in selling price expectations (16.2 vs 17.3).

### 2026-05-28 17:16:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Economic Optimism Index #Importance 2

Eurozone Economic Sentiment Inches Up in May — The Eurozone Economic Sentiment Indicator (ESI) rose slightly to 93.5 in May 2026, remaining close to the over five-year low of 93.2 recorded in April but exceeding market expectations of 92.8. Sentiment stayed weak, reflecting mounting concerns over the economic outlook amid the escalating Iran conflict. Among sectors, confidence improved slightly for service providers (2.2 vs. 1.4 in April) and consumers (-19.0 vs. -20.6), but deteriorated further for manufacturers (-8.0 vs. -7.7), retailers (-10.9 vs. -10.0), and constructors (-3.6 vs. -2.8). On the pricing front, consumer inflation expectations fell to 40.5 from a four-year high of 48.8, while manufacturers’ selling price expectations eased to 27.4 from April’s over three-year high of 30.2. In the largest Eurozone economies, the ESI increased in Germany (+1.0) and France (+0.9), remained broadly stable in Italy (+0.1) and Spain (-0.1), and declined in the Netherlands (-0.9).

Montenegro Tourist Arrivals Down 8.4% in April — The number of tourist arrivals in Montenegro fell by 8.4% year-on-year to 107,939 in April 2026, reversing a rise of 2.4% in March, as arrivals continue to increase since the beggining of the year. Foreign arrivals remain the leading source of arrivals, at 89.3%, while domestic arrivals only account for 10.7%. Europe continued to be the largest source region (83.4%), namely from Germany (11.8%), Serbia (10.7%), France (8.6%) and the UK (8%). Outside of Europe,the highest numbers of arrivals came from China (including Hong Kong) (4.8%) and the US (3.7%). By type of accomodation, seaside resorts remained the most popular destination (79.8%), followed by the capital city with 13.3%. Meanwhile, overnight stays in collective accommodation totaled 278 thousand,with foreign visitors accounting for 86.3% of stays and domestic tourists making up the remaining 13.7%.

### 2026-05-28 17:14:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Iceland Inflation Rate at 5-Month Low — The annual inflation rate in Iceland eased to 5.1% year-on-year in May 2026 from 5.2% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since December 2025. Price pressures eased across several categories, including housing and utilities (6.6% vs. 6.7% in April), food and non-alcoholic beverages (4.2% vs. 4.9%), recreation, sports and culture (2.7% vs. 3.0%), restaurants and accommodation services (5.1% vs. 5.4%), information and communication (2.9% vs. 3.4%), health (4.4% vs. 4.5%), and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.5% vs. 3.6%). Meanwhile, deflation deepened slightly for furnishings, household equipment, and routine household maintenance (-0.4% vs. -0.2%). By contrast, inflation accelerated in transportation (7.9% vs. 7.6%) and in personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods and services (6.6% vs. 5.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices slowed by 0.1% in May from a 0.8% increase in the prior month.

### 2026-05-28 17:13:16  #宏觀 #市場 #觀點

【廣期所出手！調整碳酸鋰期貨持倉限額】5月28日，廣州期貨交易所（簡稱“廣期所”）釋出修改《廣州期貨交易所碳酸鋰期貨、期權業務細則》的通知，對碳酸鋰期貨合約的一般月份持倉限額進行調整：自碳酸鋰期貨LC2610合約起，自合約掛牌之日起至交割月前1月第9個交易日（即一般月），合約單邊持倉規模6萬手以上時持倉限額為合約持倉的5%，合約單邊持倉規模6萬手以內時持倉限額為3000手。中信建投期貨分析師張維鑫表示，此次調整是將合約單邊持倉規模3萬手以內時持倉限額3000手，調整為合約單邊持倉規模6萬手以內時持倉限額為3000手；合約單邊持倉規模3萬手以上時持倉限額為合約持倉的10%，調整為合約單邊持倉規模6萬手以上時持倉限額為合約持倉的5%，可以理解為對持倉限額有所縮小。而產業客戶可以根據經營需要申請套保持倉額度，此次持倉限額調整不影響產業客戶參與。創元期貨分析師餘爍指出，該調整適用於LC2610之後的合約，投資者需要對自身持倉做好管理。廣期所相關負責人表示，交易所在充分評估品種執行情況的基礎上，結合市場發展需求和“強監管、防風險、促高質量發展”工作要求，對碳酸鋰期貨一般月份持倉限額進行了調整，旨在保障期貨市場平穩執行。同時，下一步，廣期所將持續做好品種規則最佳化，加強風險評估和市場監管，著力保障期貨市場安全平穩執行。

【日本欲借菲律賓插手南海】日本國會參議院5月27日透過“國家情報會議”設立法案，這種強化情報機構的做法和二戰前的情況如出一轍，日本在突破“和平憲法”約束、加速軍事擴張的道路上又邁出危險一步。據在日本的總檯記者李衛兵觀察，日本首相高市早苗與到訪的菲律賓總統馬科斯將於今晚（5月28日）會談，重點聚焦殺傷性武器出口問題，另據日本媒體披露，日菲首腦會談後將發表的聯合宣告草案已經曝光。草案內容顯示，雙方將對東海和南海地區局勢表達“嚴重關切”，這充分暴露了日本高市早苗政權試圖藉助菲律賓插手南海、擴張在亞太地區的軍事影響力，從而突破戰後國際社會對日本的軍事限制，使日本成為所謂“正常國家”的戰略意圖。 日本前眾議院議員小澤一郎在社交媒體上表示，在國家情報局的框架下，存在政權可能為所欲為的風險。二戰前和戰爭中的日本就是典型例子。日本民眾也連日舉行集會，抗議高市早苗政權的擴軍修憲動向。他們表示，設立“國家情報局”將壓制國內反戰聲音，是高市政權一系列危險動向中的一環，這些動向正在一步步把日本變成一個“可以發動戰爭的國家”，必須堅決反對。（CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-05-28 16:59:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Exports YoY #Importance 1

Hong Kong Exports Hit New Record High — Exports from Hong Kong soared 42.9% year-on-year to a record high of $620.9 billion in April 2026, accelerating from a 35.8% increase in the previous month. It marked the strongest annual growth since January 2021, driven mainly by robust shipments of electrical machinery, apparatus and appliances, and electrical parts thereof (49.5% vs 47.9% in March), office machines and automatic data processing machines (41.5% vs 5.0%), and miscellaneous manufactured articles (48.3% vs 28.4%). In addition, exports rebounded sharply for non-metallic mineral manufactures (57.5% vs -1.7%) and photographic apparatus, equipment and supplies, optical goods, watches and clocks (20.3% vs -4.1%). Shipments to Asia climbed 43.7%, led by Mainland China (40.7% vs 39.5%), Vietnam (69.3% vs 29.9%), and Taiwan (72.7% vs 50.9%). In contrast, export growth to the US slowed markedly to 37.5% from 80.8% in March 2026.

The design uses a minimally invasive endovascular route to deliver a signal‑collection stent to targeted brain regions without craniotomy. Decoded neural signals will be converted into motor control commands to drive a lightweight exoskeleton robot, aimed at assisting stroke and spinal‑cord injury patients and enabling closed‑loop neurofunctional rehabilitation. The partners plan to begin clinical trials within one year.

【German EV Swap Rate Hits Record; STATE AID Key Driver】Germany's rate of drivers switching from internal-combustion cars to EVs hit a record in Q1 after the government introduced a subsidy of up to €6,000 (about $6,967) per new car, insurer HUK-COBURG said on Thursday. The share swapping rose to 7.5% from 6.3% in the prior quarter, the highest since the survey began in early 2020. About one-fifth of respondents said the new STATE AID directly influenced their purchase decision and just over 10% said the subsidy made them consider an EV for the first time. Chancellor MERZ's coalition has introduced a €3bn purchase-and-lease incentive for zero-emission vehicles running from the start of this year through 2029, aimed mainly at middle- and low-income households.

Hong Kong Trade Deficit at 3-Month Low — Hong Kong’s trade deficit widened to HKD 29.5 billion in April 2026 from HKD 16 billion in the same month a year earlier, though it remained the smallest gap since January. Exports surged 42.9% year-on-year to a record HKD 620.9 billion, driven by strong shipments of electrical machinery, apparatus and appliances, and electrical parts thereof (49.5%) and telecommunications and sound recording and reproducing apparatus and equipment (54.6%). Exports to Asia jumped 43.7%, while shipments to other major destinations also rose sharply, including the US (37.5%) and the UK (88.8%). Meanwhile, imports climbed 44.4% to HKD 650.4 billion, supported by higher purchases of electrical machinery, apparatus and appliances, and electrical parts thereof (46.8%), telecommunications and sound recording and reproducing apparatus and equipment (62.1%), and miscellaneous manufactured articles (95.4%). Imports increased most from Mainland China (46.8%), the UK (80.5%), and South Korea (117.4%).

### 2026-05-28 16:41:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Portugal Consumer Confidence at 2024-Lows — The consumer confidence index in Portugal fell to -27.1 in May 2026, down from -23.9 in April, marking the lowest level since January 2024. The deterioration in sentiment was broad-based regarding the last 12 months, the index measuring the increase in prices rose to 63.2 from 57.2 last month, while household sentiment weakened to -15.5 from -12.7 and views on the country’s economic situation declined to -50.7 from -43.8. Looking ahead, expectations for countrie’s economy also deteriorated to -48.5 from -42.2, amid rising concerns over prices (66.4 vs 61.1) and unemployment (27.7 vs 25.3). In contrast, uncertainty regarding future household financial conditions improved slightly to -18.1 from -20.8.

### 2026-05-28 16:40:58  #行業 #公司 #焦點

【京滬首次聯合對三大外賣平臺企業開展行政指導】5月28日，記者從北京市市場監管局獲悉，京滬兩市市場監管局簽訂《網路餐飲第三方平臺食品安全治理合作框架協議》，建立網路餐飲食品安全協同治理機制。

兩地市場監管部門在資料共享、資訊互通、風險聯控、執法聯動等領域全面加強協作，包括共同推動外賣平臺落實“線上稽核+線下核驗”雙軌制；共同督促外賣平臺將入網餐飲商戶的食品經營許可等經營資質資訊與各省級市場監管部門掌握的對應資料進行核驗比對；共同推動外賣平臺建立統一的食品安全違法行為快速處置措施，實現“一地發現、兩地聯動、全網處置”，建立跨區域治理協作會商、日常聯絡協調、同步約談及行政指導、資訊定期通報等4項制度。

為有效落實將於6月1日施行的《網路餐飲服務經營者落實食品安全主體責任監督管理規定》，北京市市場監管局、上海市市場監管局於簽訂協議當日，聯合召開外賣平臺企業行政指導會，指導美團（註冊地位於北京）、淘寶閃購（註冊地位於上海）、京東外賣（註冊地位於上海）三大外賣平臺企業完善細化落實舉措，嚴格落實食品安全主體責任。

京滬市場監管部門現場對《網路餐飲服務經營者落實食品安全主體責任監督管理規定》進行了細緻解讀，明確劃定了平臺經營的“紅線”與“底線”，並就平臺嚴格入網資質實質性稽核、規範線上資訊公示、加強餐品配送管理、健全食品安全管理制度等方面提出了具體行政指導意見，進一步壓實平臺主體責任，築牢網路餐飲食品安全底線。

京滬市場監管部門將在市場監管總局指導下，持續加強網路餐飲食品安全協同治理，加大網路餐飲食品安全跨區域監管執法力度，聯動處置網路餐飲食品安全違法行為，全力守護好百姓“舌尖上的安全”。

### 2026-05-28 16:22:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #PCE Price Index Monthly Change #Importance 2

US PCE Inflation Likely to Remain Elevated — The US PCE price index is expected to rise 0.5% month-over-month in April 2026, following a 0.7% increase in March, which marked the sharpest gain since June 2022 amid a surge in energy costs triggered by the war with Iran. The core PCE index, which excludes food and energy, is projected to increase 0.3%, matching the pace recorded in the previous month. On an annual basis, headline PCE inflation is expected to accelerate for a second straight month to 3.8% from 3.5% in March, reaching its highest level since May 2023 and aligning with headline CPI inflation. Core PCE inflation is also seen edging higher to 3.3% from 3.2%, marking its highest reading since late 2023. Overall, the report is likely to reinforce the view that inflationary pressures remain elevated and well above the Federal Reserve’s 2% target.

Italy Business Confidence Unchanged in May — Italy's manufacturing confidence indicator remained unchanged at 87.9 in May 2026, slightly above expecations of 87.5. Sentiment improved regarding total order assessments (-18.4 vs -19.4) and production levels (-16.5 vs -16.7). Meanwhile, expectations for price growth increased (25.4 vs 25.0), reflecting ongoing energy price pressures, as expectations weakened for both production (0.5 vs 0.6) and employment (-0.8 vs 0.0). On a broader level, overall business confidence declined to 94.1 from 95.1 in the previous month). Confidence deteriorated in the construction sector (99.4 vs 100.8) and market services (96.8 vs 98.9), while retail trade sentiment improved slightly to 101.0 from 100.8.

UK Gilt Yields Slightly Up as US-Iran Tensions Reignite Inflation Fears — UK 10-year gilt yields edged up to 4.86% as escalating US-Iran tensions threatened their ceasefire, pushing oil prices higher and reigniting inflation concerns. This ended a five-day rally for UK government bonds, the longest since just before the Iran conflict escalated in late February. Recent weak labor market data, softer-than-expected inflation, and signs of slowing economic activity had led traders to scale back their bets on a Bank of England rate hike, with markets fully pricing in just one increase this year. However, traders slightly raised their bets today as rising oil prices underscored that the crisis is far from over, with a peace deal still out of reach. A Washington official confirmed US strikes on an Iranian drone operation near the Strait of Hormuz, following reports that President Donald Trump had rejected a potential compromise with Tehran. In retaliation, Iran’s Revolutionary Guard targeted a US airbase on Thursday.

【山東省臨沂市人大常委會原副主任劉紀民被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站5月28日通報，據山東省紀委監委訊息：經山東省委批准，山東省紀委監委對臨沂市人大常委會原黨組成員、副主任劉紀民嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，劉紀民喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，違規收受禮品、消費卡，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；違反組織原則，個人違規決定重大事項，隱瞞不報個人有關事項，違規為他人謀取人事利益；廉潔底線失守，向管理和服務物件放貸獲取大額回報；不重視家風建設；公權私用、搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人在工作調動、企業經營等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。劉紀民嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經山東省紀委常委會會議研究並報山東省委批准，決定給予劉紀民開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其享受的待遇；終止其臨沂市第十四次黨代會代表資格；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

【京東外賣首發行業最嚴堂食標準，啟動“堂食看得見”行動】28日，京東黑板報公眾號發文稱，在新規的指導下，京東外賣正式啟動“堂食看得見”行動，釋出行業最嚴堂食標準，這不僅是為了符合新規要求，更是為了保障消費者的權益。京東外賣制定了行業最嚴格的堂食/非堂食認定標準。只有擁有穩定持續的、與後廚隔離的就餐區域，且就餐區面積佔比大於50%的門店，才會被認定為堂食餐廳。我們絕不允許“黑外賣”擺一張桌子就能洗白成堂食店。基於此標準，我們將於6月1日全面上線打標。我們很高興地看到，京東外賣採用行業最嚴的認定標準，在非即提門店中，超過97%都是“看得見”的堂食餐廳。針對平臺上佔比極低且合規經營的無堂食門店，我們匹配嚴格的“後廚直播 + AI巡檢”雙重管控，同時疊加更嚴格的人工複核、隨機抽檢和騎手巡檢。京東外賣無堂食門店只有不足3%，是行業內無堂食門店比例最低的平臺。（京東黑板報）

### 2026-05-28 15:53:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Slovakia Business Morale Moderates in May — The industrial confidence indicator in Slovakia fell by 1.6 points month-on-month to 0.7 in May 2026, following a four-month high of 2.3 in the previous month. Expectations for future production weakened (26 vs 31 in April), primarily in the manufacture of basic pharmaceutical products. Views on stocks of finished products also eased (16 vs 22). At the same time, assessments of current order levels continued to deteriorate (-8 vs -2), reflecting weaker anticipated output in the production of coke and refined petroleum products, as well as the manufacture of other non-metallic products and machinery and equipment n.e.c. Meanwhile, assessments of current industrial production turned positive (2 vs -2), while the employment outlook improved slightly (-1 vs -6). Sentiment toward the current level of export orders remained unchanged at -14.

French Stocks Fall on Fresh US-Iran Tensions — The CAC 40 fell 0.5% to 8,168 on Thursday, reversing gains from the previous session as investors turned cautious on renewed US-Iran tensions despite ongoing peace talks. Domestically, French producer prices rose 2.1% year-on-year in April 2026, accelerating sharply from a flat reading in March and marking the highest reading since June 2023. ### 2026-05-28 15:33:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Norway #GDP Growth Non Oil Sector #Importance 0

Norway Mainland GDP Steady at 0.2% — Norway’s mainland GDP, which excludes the petroleum and offshore shipping sectors, expanded by 0.2% quarter-on-quarter in the three months to March 2026, matching the revised growth rate from the previous quarter but coming in slightly below market expectations of 0.3%. The modest expansion reflected weaker domestic demand, as household consumption contracted by 1.1% after a 1.3% gain in Q4 2025, while government spending remained steady at 0.2%. Meanwhile, gross fixed capital formation declined by 6.0%, reversing from a 6.2% expansion in the previous quarter. On the external side, exports fell by 1.4% following a 3.5% rise in Q4, while imports dropped 1.1% after increasing 3.0% previously, indicating softer trade activity during the quarter. On an annual basis, mainland GDP grew by 2.6% in Q1 2026, easing from 2.8% in the previous period.

【日方在太空領域謀求軍事鬆綁逆歷史潮流、違時代】5月28日下午，國防部舉行例行記者會，國防部新聞發言人蔣斌大校答記者問。

記者：據報道，日本防衛省公佈加強太空領域防禦能力相關措施，還宣稱已啟用低軌軍事偵察衛星星座。請問對此有何評論？

蔣斌：近年來，日本在軍事擴張的道路上越走越遠，一步步突破“和平憲法”約束，大肆佈局太空軍事力量。日本執政當局持續擴大經費投入，勾連他國開展太空聯合軍演，構建太空軍事情報體系，還打著防禦旗號謀求發展和部署太空武器，導致太空武器化和太空軍備競賽風險上升，對全球戰略穩定造成負面影響。

和平利用太空是全人類的共識。日方在太空領域謀求軍事鬆綁、重走軍國主義老路的圖謀逆歷史潮流，違時代大勢，國際社會對此應高度警惕、強力遏制，堅決維護人類的共同家園。

FTSE 100 Snaps Winning Streak as Miners Weigh — The FTSE 100 traded 0.9% lower on Thursday, snapping an eight-session winning streak and underperforming other European markets as weakness in miners and utilities outweighed gains in energy stocks. Sentiment was pressured after the US carried out air strikes on targets in Iran, driving oil prices higher and underscoring the fragility of recent peace talk momentum. Mining stocks led declines, with Fresnillo and Endeavour Mining falling more than 3%, while Antofagasta, Rio Tinto and Anglo American also moved lower. Utilities were also under pressure, with National Grid down more than 2.5% and Severn Trent losing 2.4%. In contrast, oil majors BP and Shell gained around 0.5% each as crude prices advanced. SSE was little changed after reporting earnings above expectations and announcing plans to increase capital expenditure to more than £5 billion, focused on networks and renewable energy projects.

Slovakia Consumer Morale Falls to Over 3-Year Low — Slovakia’s consumer confidence indicator dropped to -28.3 in May 2026 from -27.4 in the previous month. ### 2026-05-28 15:20:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Dutch Business Mood Weakest in 9 Months — Business confidence in the Dutch manufacturing sector deteriorated to -2.0 in May 2026 from -0.7 in April, marking the weakest reading since last August. Expected activity dropped sharply to 4.6 from 8.4, signalling a notable weakening in the near-term production outlook, while inventory judgments worsened to -5.2 from -3.3, indicating a higher perceived stock overhang. By contrast, order position assessments improved slightly to -5.6 from -7.1, suggesting some easing in demand conditions. By sector, sentiment weakened broadly. The food and beverage industry declined (-3.0 vs -1.9), wood and building materials edged lower (-3.5 vs -3.3), while refineries and chemicals weakened further (-3.7 vs -1.6), alongside base metals and metal products (-4.1 vs -0.8). Assessments were less negative for paper and graphic products (-4.0 vs -4.9), and textile, clothing, and leather (-2.9 vs -7.9).

TAIEX Pulls Back from Record Peak, Ends 1.4% Lower — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, dropped 620 points, or 1.4%, to close at 43,636 on Thursday, erasing gains from the previous session and mainly weighed down by electronic technology and technology services stocks amid profit-taking. The broader index retreated from the record high touched a day earlier, when it closed at 44,257 after surpassing the 44,000 level for the first time, mainly supported by a surge in electronic technology stocks amid optimism over AI demand. Electronic technology and technology services stocks fell 1.5% and 1.9%, respectively. Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company (TSMC), which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, dropped 0.2%, while MediaTek and Delta Electronics plunged 5.0% and 5.2%, respectively, and Hon Hai Precision Industry declined 0.4%. However, renewed escalation in the Middle East conflict further pressured sentiment following US strikes on Iranian targets.

"SpaceX did not commit to renting Colossus for years, though that could indeed happen," Musk said. Earlier this year SpaceX reached a deal with ANTHROPIC under which ANTHROPIC would pay $1.25 bln per month to use the Colossus and Colossus II clusters in Memphis, Tennessee; that arrangement was reported as running through May 2029. In a regulatory filing last week SpaceX said both parties could terminate the agreement with 90 days' notice; that filing did not mention a six-month lease. Musk said the deal is a 180-day lease, after which either side can give 90 days' notice, "We will not strand them, and will provide a reasonable exit plan, but if compute resources become extremely tight, I have said we may need at some point to reclaim those resources." ### 2026-05-28 15:18:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Swedish Consumer Morale Improves in May — Sweden’s household confidence indicator rose to 92.4 in May 2026, picking up from a revised eight-month low of 91.7 in the previous month. Households became more optimistic about their finances over the next twelve months (7 vs 5 in April), while sentiment toward the broader Swedish economy remained unchanged (-23). Concerns about unemployment over the same period also eased (26 vs 32), and attitudes toward purchasing capital goods turned less pessimistic (-15 vs -17). On the other hand, expectations for personal savings in the coming year edged lower (46 vs 47). On prices, households expected lower inflation over the next twelve months (7.4% vs 7.5%), while their assessment of inflation over the past year moderated (10.2% vs 11.2%). Meanwhile, the variable mortgage interest rate is expected to stand at 3.8% over both the one- and two-year horizons, while the five-year expectation is slightly lower at 3.7%.

He said Lai, who previously openly called himself a "practical Taiwan independence activist," is now pretending there is no "Taiwan independence" issue as a rhetorical disguise after being harshly disproved by reality; this, Jiang said, shows that pursuing "Taiwan independence" has reached a dead end and that the illusion of "seeking independence by relying on the US" will ultimately be shattered.

Sweden Business Confidence Holds Firm in May — Sweden’s business confidence indicator was broadly unchanged at 103.3 in May 2026 from 103.5 in April, remaining above the 100 mark and continuing to signal stronger-than-normal business sentiment. Demand conditions remained weak, with the indicator slipping to -24 from -23, staying below historical norms. Employment trends were largely stable, as firms reported only a slight decline in staffing over the past three months (-1 vs -2), while hiring expectations for the next three months eased to 1 from 3, pointing to a broadly unchanged workforce outlook. Construction was the only sector expecting employment growth ahead. Meanwhile, pricing intentions strengthened notably, with expected sales prices over the next three months rising sharply to 23 from 16, the highest among the surveyed indicators. The increase was particularly evident in the grocery trade, partly reflecting adjustments following the VAT reduction on food introduced in April.

【Beijing To Draft AI Industry Development Regulations, Plans First Reading In November】On May 28, the 24th meeting of the 16th Beijing Municipal People’s Congress Standing Committee heard and reviewed a report from the Municipal Ethnic, Religious, Overseas Chinese and Foreign Affairs Committee on the review outcomes of deputies’ proposals referred by the presidium of the 4th session of the 16th Municipal People’s Congress. The presidium referred four deputies’ proposals to that committee for review: (1) a proposal by Huang Leping and 12 other deputies (13 total) to draft the Regulations on the Development of Beijing’s Artificial Intelligence Industry; (2) a proposal by Yang Qiang and 14 other deputies (15 total) to draft Legislation to Promote Beijing’s Artificial Intelligence Industry Development; (3) a proposal by Tao Qinghua and 11 other deputies (12 total) to draft Legislation to Accelerate Artificial Intelligence Industry Development and Strengthen Talent Team Building; and (4) a proposal by Zhang Xiyong and 16 other deputies (17 total) to draft Legislation to Accelerate the Embodied Intelligent Robotics Industry. Under the legislative work plan, the Standing Committee intends to conduct the first reading of the draft Regulations in November this year; prior to that, the Standing Committee directors’ meeting will study the project justification report for the Beijing AI Industry Development Regulations.

### 2026-05-28 14:59:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Non Farm Payrolls #Importance 1

Swiss Non-Farm Payrolls Up 0.5% in Q1 — Switzerland’s non–farm payrolls rose by 0.5% year-on-year to 5.537 million in Q1 2026, accelerating from a 0.2% gain in the previous quarter. The increase was primarily driven by the services sector, rising 0.6% to 4.409 million, supported by higher employment in administrative and support service activities (2.5% vs 2.3%), public administration (1.7% vs 2.6%), and arts, entertainment and recreation (1.6% vs 1.1%). Declines were seen in publishing, audiovisual and broadcasting activities (-5% vs -9.3%), telecommunications (-2.3% vs -4.9%), and finance and assurance (-1.5% vs -1.9%). Meanwhile, employment in the industrial sector recovered by 0.1% to 1.129 million, after a 0.1% fall in Q4, driven by increases in mining and quarrying (3.3% vs 4.4%), electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (2.9% vs 3.6%), construction (1.3% vs 1.1%), which offset declines in manufacturing (-0.7% vs -1%) and water supply, waste management (-0.8% vs 1.9%).

### 2026-05-28 14:56:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 2

French Producer Prices Post Biggest Drop in a Year — French domestic producer prices fell 2.1% month-on-month in April 2026, reversing a revised 1.9% increase in March and marking the steepest decline since April last year. The drop was largely driven by a sharp decline in prices for mining, quarrying, energy, and water (-10.9% vs 0.7% in March), mainly due to a steep fall in electricity prices (-22.1% vs -0.2%) amid lower wholesale market prices, stronger renewable energy production, and weaker demand linked to mild temperatures. This was partially offset by a pickup in gas prices (6.5% vs 2.4%), linked to the conflict in the Middle East. Meanwhile, price growth for manufactured products slowed sharply (0.7% vs 2.3%), as food products, beverages, and tobacco declined (-0.3% vs 0.2%). Prices for coke and refined petroleum products also decelerated markedly (4.1% vs 53.1%), as oil prices eased in April following the March spike. On an annual basis, domestic producer prices rose 2.1%, after being flat in March.

【國際能源署：中東衝突影響各國佈局 能源安全再成焦點】當地時間5月28日，國際能源署（IEA）釋出的《2026年世界能源投資報告》指出，中東衝突帶來的深遠影響，正促使各國政府和企業重新審視能源投資戰略。在霍爾木茲海峽運輸受阻背景下，各方對能源安全以及全球貿易流通可靠性的擔憂顯著上升。報告稱，由霍爾木茲海峽事實上關閉所引發的當前能源危機，正在改變市場對風險的認知，並推動各國進一步尋求能源和運輸路線多元化。這場危機距離2022年俄烏衝突引發的能源危機僅過去數年，預計將對未來全球能源投資重點產生長期影響，尤其是在亞洲和中東地區。上述地區受到霍爾木茲海峽航運中斷影響最為明顯。國際能源署執行幹事法提赫·比羅爾表示：“我們正處於世界有史以來最大規模的能源安全危機之中。我認為，這將重塑全球能源投資戰略，類似於20世紀70年代石油危機後能源行業經歷的重大變化。”（央視新聞）

### 2026-05-28 14:55:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Hungary Jobless Rate at 4.5% in February-April — The unemployment rate in Hungary edged up to 4.5% in February–April 2026 from 4.4% in the corresponding period a year ago. The number of unemployed individuals rose by 1.9 thousand year-on-year to 226.4 thousand. Of the total unemployed, 116.1 thousand were men, with an unemployment rate of 4.5%, while 102.5 thousand were women, at 4.5%. Moreover, the average time spent looking for a job was 13.1 months. Among unemployed individuals, 33.3% had been seeking work for less than three months, while 30.6% had been looking for work for 4–11 months. The proportion of those searching for work for more than a year increased by 2.4 percentage points to 36.1%. Meanwhile, the number of employed persons decreased by 55 thousand year-on-year to 4.618 million. Employment among men fell by 18 thousand to 2.464 million, while employment among women declined by 36.9 thousand to 2.154 million.

【長江證券劉元瑞：金融業的本質是長期主義 “沒有哪家偉大機構靠追逐熱點成就”】5月28日，在長江證券2026年中期策略會上，長江證券黨委副書記、總裁劉元瑞分享了他對金融行業本質的深度思考：“金融和所有行業一樣，本質上都是長期主義。”劉元瑞從三個維度闡釋了這一判斷：銀行經營的是信用，信用的建立需要十年如一日的穩健；資管經營的是信任，信任的積累來自長期穿越週期的表現；投研經營的是判斷，聲譽的鑄就來自一個又一個紮實研究的沉澱。他直言，“沒有哪家偉大的金融機構，是靠追逐熱點成就的。”“真正有價值的東西，值得用時間去澆灌。”劉元瑞引用巴菲特的話“想想你希望自己的訃告寫什麼，然後去過配得上它的生活”，指出長期主義不只是投資方法，更是一種人生選擇。

【Alibaba DAMO Releases GPU Version Of MindOpt, Says It Overcomes 'Long‑Tail' Bottleneck And Can Solve 100‑Million‑Variable LPs】Alibaba DAMO Academy said on its official Weibo account today that its MindOpt solver has formally released a GPU version, leveraging GPU parallel acceleration and new algorithms to address the "long‑tail effect". Tests on about 2,000 general benchmark instances show MindOpt can stably solve more than 99% of problem types to high precision and can support linear programming problems at the 100‑million‑variable scale that were traditionally considered "unsolvable." The solver is described as "the core of industrial software," handling complex computations for power dispatch, flight scheduling, advanced manufacturing and financial management. By contrast, traditional linear programming solvers are CPU‑designed and rely on complex matrix decomposition; as problem scale grows, memory requirements can explode and limited algorithmic parallelism can leave problems unconverged for hours or cause outright crashes.

【Guangzhou Aerospace Town Phase I Breaks Ground, Accelerating Commercial Space Industry Layout In Greater Bay Area】As China’s commercial space industry accelerates toward large-scale development, the Guangdong–Hong Kong–Macao Greater Bay Area has added another strategic industrial layout. Invested in and developed by Guangzhou Construction Group, Phase I of the Guangzhou Aerospace Town project broke ground today (28th) on Xiji Island in Huangpu District, Guangzhou. The site covers 574,100 sq m at a key node of the Guangzhou–Shenzhen–Hong Kong–Macao Science and Technology Innovation Corridor, is riverfront with port access, and will include two 5,000-ton-class berths to support transport of large aerospace equipment and high-end manufacturing. ASX 200 Under Pressure at Close — The ASX 200 tumbled 125 points or 1.4% to end at 8,593 on Thursday, reversing strength in the prior session and marking a one-week low. Sentiment was hit by fresh data showing Australia's household spending fell for the first time in four months in April, highlighting the impact of the RBA’s three interest rate hikes since the start of 2026. Meanwhile, U.S. stock futures moved lower amid the fragile ceasefire between Washington and Tehran following fresh U.S. strikes on an Iranian military site. Still, a solid rebound in Q1 capital expenditure helped limit losses, mainly lifted by stronger spending on plant machinery. In addition, softer-than-expected April inflation eased concerns that the RBA could maintain a tightening bias. Losses were broadly spread, led by falls in mining, consumer, financial, and healthcare stocks. The four major banks dropped between 1.4% and 2.2%. Other notable laggards included BHP Group (-1.3%), Northern Star Resources (-7.4%), and Evolution Mining (-7.3%).

【機構：5月全球顯示器面板價格延續漲勢 預計主流規格環比漲幅溫和收窄】綜合供需兩端及成本因素，群智諮詢(Sigmaintell)預測，5月份顯示器面板主流面板價格漲幅環比將小幅收窄，中高階面板維持平穩走勢。5月全球顯示器面板價格延續漲勢，主流規格環比漲幅溫和收窄，行業進入供需博弈關鍵期。在供需結構性偏緊、上游材料價格持續上漲，面板供應集中度進一步提升的共同作用下，顯示器面板市場仍將延續價格上行趨勢。然而，受半導體市場持續漲價影響，顯示器整機儲存相關部件價格也在持續上漲，非屏部件成本的上漲逐步擠壓面板漲價空間，疊加TV面板價格全面止漲，品牌方對於顯示器面板主流規格價格漲幅收窄訴求強烈。截至5月末，基於整機成本壓力加大以及前置備貨需求基本完成，部分品牌面板採購需求邊際走弱，5月份將成為供需雙方價格博弈關鍵期。

### 2026-05-28 14:29:30  #MKT News #Important #Market Themes #Chip Wars #AMD.O #INTC.O

【ByteDance Said To Be Developing Custom CPU To Support AI Demand】People familiar with the matter said ByteDance is developing its own CPU to meet growing AI infrastructure demand, citing a surge in chip prices and prolonged supply shortages that have constrained the company’s expansion plans. The move highlights an industry shift into the "inference" phase of AI, which has materially increased CPU demand and precipitated shortages in recent months, the sources said. ByteDance plans to deploy the in‑house CPUs in its servers and data centers to support internal operations and to prepare for large‑scale rollouts of agent‑like products including the Coze platform. The company is pursuing two CPU architecture tracks — one Arm‑based and one based on the open‑source RISC‑V instruction set — to assess which design better suits its long‑term data center needs. ByteDance currently buys CPUs from Intel and AMD; both suppliers have hiked prices sharply in recent months, with QoQ increases of roughly 10%–35%, prompting the company to accelerate development of an internal alternative, the people said.

Lithuania Retail Sales Growth at 3-Month High — Retail sales in Lithuania rose 8.9% year-on-year in April 2026 from an upwardly revised 6.7% in the previous month and marking the strongest growth since January. Sales growth accelerated in retail trade excluding automotive fuel (11.4% vs. 6.8% in March), non-food products (18.8% vs. 10.1%), mail-order and internet sales (34.6% vs. 21.1%), and non-specialized stores (25.2% vs. 18.3%). In specialized stores, retail activity also expanded for textiles, clothing, and footwear (7.6% vs. 6.1%), audio, video, hardware, furniture, and lighting (21.1% vs. 7.1%), information and communication goods (7.2% vs. 2.4%), and pharmaceuticals, medical goods, and cosmetics (13.1% vs. 9.0%). In contrast, sales growth slowed for food, alcoholic beverages, and tobacco (1.5% vs. 2.2%), while automotive fuel sales declined (-0.9% vs. 6.6%). On a monthly basis, retail activity eased 1.8% in April from an upwardly revised 3.7% in the previous period.

Brent Climbs on Renewed US-Iran Tensions — Brent crude futures climbed toward $97 per barrel on Thursday, rebounding from losses in the previous session as renewed hostilities between the US and Iran weakened expectations for a near-term peace agreement that could end the conflict and reopen the Strait of Hormuz. US forces reportedly struck an Iranian military site believed to pose a threat to American troops and commercial shipping routes through Hormuz, while also intercepting Iranian drones. Meanwhile, Iran’s Revolutionary Guard said it had targeted a US airbase, though it did not disclose the location. Washington and Tehran also remained at odds over major sticking points in negotiations, including Iran’s insistence on maintaining control of the strait and preserving its nuclear program. Despite the latest escalation, oil prices are still on track for a second consecutive weekly decline amid expectations that both sides could eventually reach a peace agreement and reopen the Strait of Hormuz.

### 2026-05-28 14:19:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Lithuania Industrial Output Hits 13-Month High — Lithuania’s industrial production rose by 7.5% year-on-year in April 2026, following a upwardly revised 5.0% growth in the previous month. This marked the highest growth in industrial activity since March 2025, supported by a rebound in mining and quarrying output (6.3% vs -4.6% in March). At the same time, manufacturing production also continued to expand, though at a slower pace (8.1% vs 10.8%), while the decline in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply eased (-4.9% vs -7.4%). Meanwhile, output in water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation services fell further (-12.0% vs -10.8%). On a monthly basis, industrial production fell by 1.6% in April, following a upwardly revised 3.3% gain in the preceding period.

### 2026-05-28 14:17:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Norway #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 2

Norwegian Economy Rebounds in Q1 — The Norwegian economy expanded by 0.4% quarter-on-quarter in Q1 2026, rebounding from an upwardly revised 0.6% contraction in the previous period. The recovery was mainly driven by an acceleration in petroleum activities and ocean transport, which grew 5.4% compared with 2.9% in Q4. Net trade also contributed positively to GDP growth, as exports rose 7.0%, unchanged from the prior quarter, while import growth eased to 3.0% from 2.5%. Meanwhile, growth in household consumption slowed to 1.9% from 4.0%, while government spending eased to 2.9% from 3.1%. Fixed investments also contracted by 1.3% after expanding 2.4% in Q4. On an annual basis, GDP expanded 1.7%, easing from revised growth of 2.0% in Q4 and marking the weakest expansion in three quarters. The GDP for Mainland Norway, which excludes the largely petroleum-based offshore sector, grew 0.5% quarter-on-quarter, sharply slowing from a 1.7% increase in the prior period.

【分析：美至少得花3年補齊伊朗戰事消耗武器】美國戰略與國際問題研究中心27日釋出的最新分析報告顯示，美國軍火承包商至少需要三年時間，才能補齊在伊朗戰事中大量使用的三種關鍵武器系統的庫存。 據美聯社報道，報告說，美國戰略與國際問題研究中心分析了美國國防部現有的公開資訊後發現，“戰斧”巡航導彈、“愛國者”和“薩德”導彈防禦系統現有彈藥庫存能夠應對伊朗戰事的“任何合理情景”。然而，補齊因此消耗的武器庫存需要時間，如果這期間美國在其他地區捲入軍事衝突，則將遭遇“脆弱視窗期”。 報告說，這個視窗期會持續數年，直到美國武器庫存恢復到對伊朗作戰之前的水平；若要達到應付新的軍事衝突的水平，則還需要再多等幾年。報告說：“擴大產能、打造這些複雜的武器系統都需要時間。”（新華社）

### 2026-05-28 14:14:15  #MKT News

【Hong Kong Sets Preliminary Tax Framework To Attract High-Value Industries, Xu Zhengyu Says】Xu Zhengyu, Secretary for Financial Services and the Treasury of the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, told the Legislative Council today (May 28) that the government will continue to solicit industry views and, as appropriate, optimize Hong Kong’s tax system. The government has drawn up a preliminary framework for tax concessions under a package of preferential policies, including offering a half-rate or a 5% preferential tax rate, aimed at attracting high value-added industries and high-potential enterprises. Firms locating in the Northern Metropolis or elsewhere in Hong Kong would be eligible to apply for the package’s preferential rates. Because the tax concessions span multiple policy bureaux and require specialist input, the government will consult relevant bureaux and departments on which industries and enterprises are covered and on sector-specific requirements. The administration plans to seek LegCo’s views later this year and to submit an amendment bill to the ordinance.

【好想來稱全國門店下架涉事NFC果汁產品】日前，央視財經曝光了市場上NFC果汁存在的標籤亂象。不少標稱“100%NFC”的果汁產品，在其配料表中，都顯示有水和濃縮果汁，這使消費者在選購產品時產生了困惑。 另據報道，僅今年以來，就有多款NFC果汁因涉嫌虛假宣傳被曝光。4月，“好想來”旗下橙汁、蘋果汁、葡萄汁等產品，包裝醒目標註“100%果汁”“NFC非濃縮還原”，但配料表前兩位均為水、濃縮果汁，NFC果汁新增量極低（每公斤僅5克），被曝光後登上微博熱搜。 5月26日，好想來品牌所屬公司萬辰集團相關負責人回應稱，針對近期報道中提及的果汁標籤問題，好想來已將全國門店所有相關商品全部下架。後續好想來將進一步強化標籤合規管理，杜絕類似情況再次發生。 同月，北冰洋一款葡萄複合果汁在正面醒目標註稱“新增NFC果汁”，但配料表卻顯示NFC葡萄汁新增量僅0.005%，品牌方回應稱產品無標註錯誤，但市場監管部門已介入調查。

### 2026-05-28 13:57:19  #公司 #國際 #原創

【市場有望走出震盪格局，重回上行趨勢，A500ETF南方（159352）獲淨申購4525萬元】5月28日，A500ETF南方（159352）探底回升，截至發稿漲0.07%，成交額29億元。資金流向方面，昨日資金不懼市場回撥，逆勢淨申購4525萬元，最新份額221.22億份，規模301億元。光大證券認為，中長期來看，多重利好因素共振發力，市場有望走出震盪格局，重回上行趨勢。一方面，雖然海外地緣緊張局勢仍在持續，相關不確定性尚未完全消散，但經過前期的調整，市場對中東局勢的邊際變化已逐步鈍化，避險情緒帶來的短期擾動持續邊際弱化。另一方面，上市公司業績逐步改善，在外部風險因素影響逐步鈍化之後，國內上市公司基本面的確定性，或將成為下一個階段支撐市場逐步上行的核心力量。華安證券表示，近期市場結構迎極致分化，AI產業鏈算力中游和配件上游的核心主線地位繼續強化，仍是中期最重要方向，但需注意電子交易熱度過高面臨降溫壓力劇增，預計很快進入有幅度、有時間的調整，而近期降溫後的通訊有望成為接力電子的最優選擇。此外儲能鏈、機械裝置、軍工等也需重視。A500ETF南方（159352）憑藉“均衡配置”的特性凸顯配置價值。該產品打破了傳統寬基指數對單一行業的過度傾斜，透過科學權重設計，將傳統週期龍頭與新興科技先鋒有機融合，實現新舊動能轉換及價值與成長風格的精準平衡。作為一站式佈局利器，投資者既能依託其高度分散且均衡的持倉結構有效平滑風格輪動帶來的波動，又可精準捕獲經濟復甦紅利，進而分享中國核心資產在全球競爭中的長期溢價。投資者可借道A500ETF南方（159352）及其聯接基金（A類022434；C類022435）一鍵佈局。

NZX 50 Reverses Morning Gains, Ends 0.2% Lower — The NZX 50 fell 22 points, or 0.2%, to close at 13,206 on Thursday, reversing morning gains and erasing advances from the previous session after touching its highest level since May 7 a day earlier, amid inflation concerns fueled by rising oil prices due to the escalating conflict in the Middle East. Traders also assessed a statement from a top central banker today that how high interest rates will go depends on how much weaker demand from higher energy costs would hold back other price increases, after the RBNZ kept interest rates unchanged on Wednesday. The governor of the RBNZ, Anna Breman, told Morning Report that the official cash rate is expected to rise to 3% or 3.25% in the future. Most sectors traded in the red, including materials, consumer discretionary, consumer staples, and financials. ANZ Group fell 2.5%, A2 Milk shrank 2.1%, Chorus declined 1.7%, Ebos Group dropped 1.6%, Westpac Banking Corp fell 1.1%, and Auckland International Airport lost 1.1%.

【Chinese Embassy In Zimbabwe Urges Chinese Firms And Citizens In Zimbabwe To Strengthen Security Precautions】On the 27th the Chinese Embassy in Zimbabwe issued a consular reminder saying that, owing to a recent series of robberies against Chinese nationals in Zimbabwe — including roadblock robberies and residential break‑ins — Chinese-funded enterprises, institutions and citizens in Zimbabwe should increase risk awareness and bolster security precautions. The embassy said Chinese citizens should closely monitor local public‑security developments and promptly follow safety notices from the embassy, overseas Chinese organizations, trade associations and the Zimbabwean government; if facing danger, they should prioritize personal safety. The embassy urged relevant enterprises and institutions to improve on‑site security measures, strengthen employee safety education and reminders, and enhance daily management and supervision of security personnel; to operate in accordance with law and compliance requirements, respect local religions and customs, handle disputes lawfully, and properly safeguard workers’ legal rights.

【CHINA FOREIGN MINISTER WANG YI Holds Talks With U.S. Strategic and Business Figures】On the 27th local time in New York, CHINA FOREIGN MINISTER WANG YI met with U.S. strategic and business figures while presiding over a UN Security Council high‑level meeting. Wang said the China‑U.S. leaders held a historic meeting in Beijing and engaged in candid, deep, constructive and strategic communications on major issues affecting China‑U.S. relations and world peace and development, reaching a series of important understandings. Both sides agreed to position a "China‑U.S. constructive strategic stability relationship" as the new orientation of bilateral ties and to work toward a relationship centered on cooperation, measured competition, controllable differences and prospective peace, jointly exploring how major powers should properly coexist in the new era. Wang stressed that "Taiwan independence" and peace and stability in the Taiwan Strait are mutually incompatible, and said only by upholding the One‑China principle and the spirit of the three China‑U.S. Joint Communiqués can peace in the Taiwan Strait be guaranteed and conflict avoided. He said China is willing to work with the U.S. to implement the important consensus between the two heads of state and to take concrete actions to bring the "constructive strategic stability relationship" into effect as soon as possible, and expressed hope that attendees would make active efforts and help build broader social consensus.

Kosovo Trade Gap at 9-Month High — Kosovo’s trade deficit widened sharply to EUR 576.4 million from EUR 492.3 million in the same month a year earlier. This marked the largest trade gap since July 2025, as imports advanced 19.2% year-on-year to EUR 674.8 million, lifted by higher purchases of organic chemicals (59%), pharmaceutical products (50.3%), and plastic and plastic products (28.1%). Kosovo’s main import partners were Germany (17.5%), Albania (16%), and Poland (59.9%). Meanwhile, exports jumped 33.3% to EUR 98.4 million, lifted by increased shipments of ceramic products (22.2%), iron and steel products (193.6%), and plastic and plastic products (21%). Export growth was mainly driven by key markets including Italy (126.6%), Switzerland (54%), and Macedonia (48%).

Estonia Retail Sales Growth Slows to 4-Month Low — Retail sales in Estonia increased by 1.6% year-on-year in April 2026 from 6.9% in the previous month. This marked the softest rise in retail activity since a decline in December 2025. Growth slowed sharply in automotive fuel sold in specialized stores, which rose 2.7% compared with 14.8% in the previous month, along with non-food products excluding fuel, which increased 1.4% from 10.1%. Meanwhile, retail activity in food, beverages, and tobacco declined by 0.9%, easing from a 1.8% drop in the prior month. On a monthly basis, retail sales fell by 3.5% in April 2026, reversing sharply after a 14.5% advance in the prior month.
